What should Smita choose to do in her class from the following suggestions? स्मिता अपने शिक्षार्थियों को कार्य से संबंधित लैंगिक विषमता और रुढ़िवादी छवियों के प्रति संवेदनशील करना चाहती है। स्मिता को निम्नलिखित में से अपनी कक्षा में क्या करना चाहिए?

- **(a)** Ask only boys to sweep the classroom daily. केवल लड़कों से प्रतिदिन कक्षा को साफ करने को कहे।
- **(b)** Invite a student's mother to class who is an auto driver./एक छात्र की माँ को कक्षा में आमंत्रित करे जो ऑटो चालक है।
- **(c)** Present students with stereotypical models. रुढ़िवादी मॉडलों को छात्रों को प्रस्तुत करें।
- **(d)** Show flashcards of different kind of work. विभिन्न प्रकार के कार्यों के फ्लैश कार्ड दिखाएँ।

**उत्तर: (b)**

एक शिक्षिका के तौर पर स्मिता को अपने शिक्षार्थियों को लैंगिक विषमता एवं रुढ़िवादी छवियों के प्रति संवेदनशील बनाने के लिए वास्तविकता पर आधारित तथ्यों को प्रस्तुत करना चाहिए। अत: स्मिता को अपनी कक्षा में उस छात्र की माँ को आमंत्रित करना चाहिए जो एक ऑटो चालक है।

### प्रश्न 89

Puneet gave his class v learners an outline of human body and asked them to draw the digestive system before teaching chapter on food. Puneet wanted to पुनीत ने अपनी कक्षा 5 के शिक्षार्थियों को मानव शरीर की एक रुपरेखा दी और उन्हें भोजन विषय पढ़ाने से पहले पाचन तंत्र को बनाने को कहा। पुनीत चाहता था

- **(a)** Identify learners with good drawing skills. अच्छे ड्राइंग कौशलों वाले शिक्षार्थियों की पहचान करना।
- **(b)** evaluate learners on the process of digestion पाचन की प्रक्रिया पर शिक्षार्थियों का मूल्यांकन करना।
- **(c)** test if learners could make labelled diagrams. परीक्षण करना कि शिक्षार्थी नामांकित आरेख बना सकता है।
- **(d)** elicit learner's ideas about digestion. पाचन से संबंधित शिक्षार्थियों के विचारों को प्राप्त करना।

**उत्तर: (d)**

अध्यापक को शिक्षार्थियों को पाठ पढ़ाने से पूर्व छात्रों से उस विषय के सन्दर्भ में पूर्वानुमान लगाने का प्रयास करना चाहिए जिससे अध्यापक को ज्ञात हो सके कि पढ़ाये जाने वाले विषय में छात्रों के पास कितनी जानकारी है। अत: भोजन विषय पढ़ाने से पहले अध्यापक को पाचन से सम्बन्धित शिक्षार्थियों के विचारों को प्राप्त करना चाहिए।

### प्रश्न 90

Which of the following is not correct with respect to formative assessment?/निम्नलिखित में से क्या रचनात्मक आकलन के संबंध में सही नहीं है?

- **(a)** It is to monitor student's progress. इसकों छात्रों की प्रगति की निगरानी करनी है
- **(b)** Any information on learning of child can help in formative assessment./बच्चे के सीखने की कोई भी जानकारी रचनात्मक आकलन में मदद कर सकती है।
- **(c)** It is to be reported in every quarter of report card /रिपोर्ट कार्ड के प्रत्येक तिमाही में इसकी सूचना दी जानी है।
- **(d)** It helps the teacher in taking timely action for enhancing learning/अधिगम को सुदृढ़ करने के लिए यह अध्यापक को समय पर कार्यवाही करने में मदद करता है।

**उत्तर: (c)**

रचनात्मक आकलन के संबंध में सही है – • इसको छात्रों की प्रगति की निगरानी करनी है। • बच्चे के सीखने की कोई भी जानकारी रचनात्मक आकलन में मदद कर सकती है। • अधिगम को सुदृढ़ करने के लिए यह अध्यापक को समय पर कार्यवाही करने में मदद करता है। अत: रचनात्मक आकलन के संबंध में रिपोर्ट कार्ड के प्रत्येक तिमाही में इसकी सूचना दी जानी सही नहीं है। क्योंकि रचनात्मक कार्यों का मूल्यांकन कभी भी किया जा सकता है। LANGUAGE-I : ENGLISH (वैकल्पिक)

## PART–IV/भाग-IV

> **निर्देश / गद्यांश:** Directions : Read the passage given below and answer the questions that follow (Q. Nos. 91 to 99), by selecting the correct/most appropriate options: 1. Each drop represents a little bit of creation and of life itself. When the monsoon brings to northern India the first rains of summer, the parched earth opens its pores and quenches its thirst with a hiss of ecstasy. After baking in the sun for the last few months, the land looks cracked, dusty and tired. Now, almost overnight, new grass springs up, there is renewal everywhere, and the damp earth releases a fragrance sweeter than any devised by man. 2. Water brings joy to earth, grass, leaf bud, blossom, insect, bird, animal and the pounding heart of man. Small children run out of their homes to romp naked in the rain. Buffaloes, which have spent the summer listlessly around lakes gone dry, now plunge into a heaven of muddy water. Soon the lakes and rivers will overflow with the monsoon's generosity. Trekking in the Himalayan foothills, I recently walked for kilometres without encountering habitation. I was just scolding myself for not having brought along a water bottle, when I came across a patch of green on a rock face. I parted a curtain of tender maiden hair fern and discovered a tiny spring issuing from the rock-nectar for the thirsty traveller. 3. I stayed there for hours, watching the water descend, drop by drop, into a tiny casement in the rocks. Each drop reflected creation. That same spring. I later discovered, joined other springs to form a swift, tumbling stream, which went cascading down the hill into other streams until, in the plains, it became part of a river. And that river flowed into another mightier river that kilometres later emptied into the ocean. Be like water, taught Laotzu, philosopher and founder of Taoism. Soft and limpid, it finds its way through, over or under any obstacle. It does not quarrel, it simply moves on.

### प्रश्न 91

Which one of the following words is most opposite in meaning to the word 'descend' (Para 3) as used in the passage?

- **(a)** hover
- **(b)** Zoom
- **(c)** flow
- **(d)** ascend

**उत्तर: (d)**

'Ascend' is the most opposite in the meaning to the word 'descend'. Descend - गिरना/नीचे उतरना, Ascend - चढ़ना/ऊपर जाना hover - मंडराना/उड़ना, zoom - गुनगुनाहट, flow - प्रवाह/अधिकता अर्थ के अनुसार Descend का सही विलोम Ascend है, इस प्रकार विकल्प (d) सही उत्तर होगा।

### प्रश्न 92

Which part of speech is the underlined word in the following sentence? Almost overnight new grass spring up.

- **(a)** Adjective
- **(b)** Adverb
- **(c)** Preposition
- **(d)** Pronoun

**उत्तर: (b)**

उपरोक्त वाक्य के underline part 'Almost' वाक्य में प्रयुक्त overnight (Adjective) को modify कर रहा है। अत: यह Adverb के रूप में कार्य कर रहा है। Note : An adverb is a word that modifies a verb, adjective, another adverb or even a whole sentence.

### प्रश्न 93

Which part of the following sentence contains an error? Heknew (A) that hewill (B) goback (C) on hispromise (D)

- **(a)** (C)
- **(b)** (D)
- **(c)** (A)
- **(d)** (B)

**उत्तर: (d)**

उपरोक्त sentence के 'B' part में error है। यहाँ पर will के स्थान पर would का प्रयोग होगा क्योंकि अगर main clause past tense में है तो subordinate clause भी past tense में ही होगी। He knew that he would go back on his promise.

### प्रश्न 94

Which of the following statements is not true?

- **(a)** The sweltering heat comes to an end.
- **(b)** The damp earth releases a sweet fragrance.
- **(c)** There is renewal everywhere.
- **(d)** New grasses spring up.

**उत्तर: (a)**

Paragraph के अनुसार option b, c, d सही हैं केवल option (a) paragraph के अनुसार सही नहीं है।

### प्रश्न 95

The earth does not look ____ before the onset of the monsoon.

- **(a)** dusty
- **(b)** tired
- **(c)** cracked
- **(d)** brown

**उत्तर: (d)**

According to the 6th line of the paragraph 1st में earth looks dusty, tired and cracked दिया हुआ है। paragraph के अनुसार 'brown' mentioned नहीं है।

### प्रश्न 96

Children respond to the first rains of summer by

- **(a)** running and playing in the rain.

### प्रश्न 99

Which of the following words is most similar in meaning to the word 'pounding' as used in para 2 of the passage?

- **(a)** Palpitating
- **(b)** sinking
- **(c)** shaking
- **(d)** benumbing

**उत्तर: (a)**

Pounding - तेजी से (दिल का जोर से धड़कना) Palpitating - दिल का जोर से धड़कना/तीव्र स्पंदन Shaking - कंपन Benumbing - स्तंभित इस प्रकार word Pounding और Palpitating paragraph 2nd के अनुसार अर्थ में सबसे अधिक समान हैं।

> **निर्देश / गद्यांश:** Directions: Read the poem given below and answer the questions that follow (Q.Nos. 100 to 105) by selecting the correct/most appropriate options: I think that I shall never see A poem lovely as a tree. A tree whose hungry mouth is prest Against the earth's sweet flowing breast; A tree that looks at God all day, and lifts her leafy arms to pray; A tree that may in Summer wear A nest of robins in her hair; Upon whose bosom snow has lain; Who intimately lives with rain. Poem are made by fools like me, But only God can make a tree.

### प्रश्न 100

Name the figure of speech used in Iines 3 and 4.

- **(a)** Metonymy
- **(b)** Alliteration
- **(c)** Simile
- **(d)** Personification

**उत्तर: (d)**

Lines 3 व 4 में mouth और breast के प्रयोग से स्पष्ट है कि वाक्य में personification, figure of speech (अलंकार) का प्रयोग हुआ है। Personification–"It is a figure of speech where non- living objects are described to seem like living objects." Personification में निर्जीव वस्तु को सजीव की भांति प्रस्तुत किया जाता है।

### प्रश्न 101

Identify and name the figure of speech used in 'Poems are made by fools like me'

- **(a)** Simile
- **(b)** Hyperbole
- **(c)** Metaphor
- **(d)** Personification

**उत्तर: (a)**

उपरोक्त line में 'like' के प्रयोग से स्पष्ट है कि वाक्य में 'Simile' figure of speech का प्रयोग हुआ है। Simile–A simile is a figure of speech that compares two different things using the words 'like', 'so' or 'as'.

line 3 के अनुसार word 'mouth' पेड़ की जड़ों (roots) को इंगित कर रहा है।

### प्रश्न 103

The tree presses its mouth against the sweet earth's flowing breast to

- **(a)** draw inspiration from it.
- **(b)** express its love for it.
- **(c)** express its gratitude to it.
- **(d)** draw sustenance from it.

**उत्तर: (d)**

line 3 के भाव के अनुसार The tree presses its mouth against the sweet earth's flowing breast to ' draw sustenance from it' सही है।

### प्रश्न 104

The tree prays to God by

- **(a)** Producing fruit and flowers
- **(b)** providing shade to travellers
- **(c)** swinging it branches
- **(d)** lifting her arms

**उत्तर: (d)**

line 5, 6 के अनुसार The tree prays to God by lifting her arms.

> **निर्देश / गद्यांश:** Directions: Answer the following questions by selecting the most appropriate options.

### प्रश्न 106

A student of class IV is having some problem in writing. She may have

- **(a)** dysphasia
- **(b)** dyscalculia
- **(c)** dyslexia
- **(d)** dysgraphia

**उत्तर: (d)**

Dysgraphia–It is a neurological disorder of written expression that impairs writing ability and fine motor skills./डिस्ग्रैफिया से प्रभावित बच्चों में लेखन प्रक्रिया संबंधी कठिनाई पायी जाती है।

### प्रश्न 107

It specifies the rules which govern the arrangement of words into phrases, clauses and sentences.

- **(a)** Syntax
- **(b)** Cohesion
- **(c)** Discourse
- **(d)** Semantics

**उत्तर: (a)**

Syntax–The arrangement of words, phrases, clauses and sentences to create well formed sentences in a language is called syntax. Syntax या वाक्य विन्यास–किसी भाषा में जिन सिद्धांतों एवं प्रक्रियाओं के द्वारा वाक्य बनते हैं, उनके अध्ययन को भाषा विज्ञान में Syntax या वाक्य विन्यास कहते हैं।

### प्रश्न 108

A teacher of class VI has labelled the classroom objects such as blackboard, door, window, fan, table, chair etc, in different languages (Hindi, English, Urdu, Sanskrit etc.) Here the classroom as a whole

- **(a)** is focusing on three language formula.
- **(b)** is a rich resource for promoting multilingualism.
- **(c)** is learning through grammar translation method.
- **(d)** is creating a language confusion in students.

**उत्तर: (b)**

Multilingualism is the ability of an individual speaker to communicate effectively in three or more languages. एक शिक्षिका द्वारा कक्षा-कक्ष में विभिन्न वस्तुओं को अलग-अलग भाषा में छात्रों के सम्मुख प्रस्तुत करना (Multilingualism) बहुभाषावाद को बढ़ावा देता है।

### प्रश्न 109

The assessment process not only assesses students learning but also assesses teacher's teaching process. This statement is

- **(a)** Partially right
- **(b)** baseless
- **(c)** fully wrong
- **(d)** fully right

**उत्तर: (d)**

The given statement the assessment process not only assesses student learning but also assesses teachers teaching process is fully right. The assessment process (मूल्यांकन प्रक्रिया) में छात्रों के साथ- साथ शिक्षक का भी मूल्यांकन होता है, यह कथन (fully right) पूर्णत: सत्य है।

### प्रश्न 110

The language which is learnt from environment without any explicit teaching is

- **(a)** Third language
- **(b)** School language
- **(c)** First language
- **(d)** Second language

**उत्तर: (c)**

First language पर्यावरण के संपर्क में स्वत: सीखी जाने वाली भाषा होती है।

### प्रश्न 111

A teacher is facing the problem of student' poor spelling. She listed the problem causes for it and then decided to frame some steps to overcome it. This whole process is

- **(a)** Action research
- **(b)** Applied research
- **(c)** Problem solving
- **(d)** Improving spelling

**उत्तर: (a)**

Action research–It is a reflexive process which helps teachers to explore and examine aspects of teaching and learning and to take action to change and improve. शिक्षण के क्षेत्र में शिक्षक के द्वारा स्वयं की कार्यप्रणाली की समस्या का चयन करने, उसका वैज्ञानिक ढंग से अध्ययन करने एवं समाधान ढूंढकर वर्तमान क्रिया में सुधार करने की प्रक्रिया को क्रियात्मक अनुसंधान Action research कहते हैं।

### प्रश्न 112

Reading picture book means

- **(a)** to help learners to draw near and colourful pictures.
- **(b)** that children love pictures and it will be a fun loving class.
- **(c)** exposing learners to different styles to drawing.
- **(d)** to help learners to understand and analyse the pictures.

**उत्तर: (d)**

Reading picture book means to help learners to understand and analyse the pictures. Basic level पर picture book का प्रयोग छात्रों में समझ व विश्लेषण विकसित करने में सहायक होती है।

### प्रश्न 113

Before starting a new chapter from a text book a teacher should at first focus on____.

- **(a)** relating the story with the previous experiences of learners.
- **(b)** the grammatical structures in the chapter and difficult words.
- **(c)** the background setting of story, its History etc.
- **(d)** the work profile of the poet/writer.

**उत्तर: (a)**

Before starting a new chapter from a text book a teacher should at first focus on relating the story with the previous experiences of learners. पाठ्य पुस्तक में एक नये chapter को start करने से पहले शिक्षक को छात्रों के पूर्व के अनुभव को पाठ्य सामग्री से जोड़कर देखना चाहिए।

### प्रश्न 114

Teacher asks learners to read aloud in the English class. This way of reading will.

- **(a)** enable learner to be a fast reader.
- **(b)** help the teacher to complete the syllabus within the stipulated times.
- **(c)** enable learners to develop reading skill with understanding.
- **(d)** enable learners to read with correct pronunciation using the punctuation marks.

**उत्तर: (d)**

Classroom में सस्वर पठन के द्वारा शिक्षक अपने छात्रों की reading skill को observe करके सही उच्चारण (pronunciation) व punctuation marks का सही प्रयोग कराने पर बल देता है।

### प्रश्न 115

A language teacher firstly, asks students to introduce themselves to each other in the class. Then she asks them to introduce their friend to each other. This activity will help them to increase.

- **(a)** PPPP
- **(b)** ELT
- **(c)** BICS
- **(d)** CALP

**उत्तर: (c)**

BICS–Basic Interpersonal Communication Skills का प्रयोग दिन प्रतिदिन की क्रियाओं व अनौपचारिक भाषा का प्रयोग करके communication skills को improve करना है।

### प्रश्न 116

Literature should be included in language classroom because

- **(a)** it develops moral values among the learners.
- **(b)** it exposes them to different genres, culture and social issues.
- **(c)** it helps learners to be budding writers.
- **(d)** it gives exposure to different grammatical from and structures.

**उत्तर: (b)**

Language classroom में literature को शामिल करना अधिगम में सहायक होगा, literature के शिक्षण से छात्रों में विभिन्न विधाओं, संस्कृति और सामाजिक मुद्दों के प्रति समझ उत्पन्न होगी।

### प्रश्न 117

A teacher makes her learners respond to her actions and speech only by bodily movements. What it this known as?

- **(a)** Communicative Approach
- **(b)** Play-way method
- **(c)** Total physical Response
- **(d)** Task Based Language Teaching

**उत्तर: (c)**

Total physical Response (TPR) is a method by teaching language or vocabulary concepts by using physical movement to react to verbal input. Total physical Response (TPR) एक शिक्षण विधि है, जिसमें मानसिक क्रियाओं के साथ-साथ छात्रों की शारीरिक क्रियाओं व गति को भी अधिगम प्रक्रिया में शामिल किया जाता है।

### प्रश्न 118

In a language class students are memorizing the dialogues through drill and practice and the teacher is correcting their pronunciation, mistakes immediately. They are practising the dialogues in chorus. The teacher is following the

- **(a)** Audio-lingual method
- **(b)** CLT method
- **(c)** Direct method
- **(d)** Natural method

**उत्तर: (a)**

The Audio-lingual method is via. teaching vocabulary and grammatical patterns through dialogues, to enable students to respond quickly and accurately in spoken language. The Audio-lingual method का प्रयोग foreign language के शिक्षण में किया जाता है। यह method listening और speaking skills में सुधार के साथ- साथ शब्दकोश व संवाद कला को भी develop करता है।

### प्रश्न 119

The oral errors of the students can be best corrected if the teacher

- **(a)** ignores the error as it may hamper their learning.
- **(b)** asks other students to help him as peer tutoring is must a schools.
- **(c)** corrects an error directly after the student has made the error.
- **(d)** points out the error in general and lets the student try to correct himself first.

**उत्तर: (d)**

छात्रों की मौखिक गलतियों को point out करके तथा छात्रों को अपनी गलती सुधार का स्वाभाविक अवसर प्रदान करना, मौखिक गलतियों को सुधारने का सबसे अच्छा तरीका माना जाता है।

### प्रश्न 120

As a pair activity the teacher gives her learners almost the same pictures and asks them to find the difference between the two students ask each other questions to discover how the two pictures are different. This can be a

- **(a)** Cloze based activity
- **(b)** Crosswords activity
- **(c)** grammar activity
- **(d)** speaking activity

**उत्तर: (d)**

Speaking activity के अंतर्गत discussions, song, play, story-telling, picture describing आदि activities शामिल होती हैं, जो छात्रों में analysis की प्रक्रिया को बढ़ावा देती हैं। LANGUAGE-II : ENGLISH (वैकल्पिक)

## PART–V/भाग-V

> **निर्देश / गद्यांश:** Directions : Read the passage given below and answer the questions that follow (Q. No. 121 to 128) by choosing the correct/most appropriate options: 1. The study of handwriting is known as graphology and it has been practised for hundreds of years. Professional forensic graphologists have worked on many court cases to use handwriting to link suspects with crimes. 2. Handwriting is particularly important legally in the case of signatures and proving whether signatures are real or forged can be pivotal. Graphologists also work to verify whether autographs are real or fake. 3. Some handwriting analysts also study writing samples to determine personality types and some businesses commission this analysis before hiring new employees. The method is even sometime used to help couples see if they are compatible. According to graphologists, there is very little you can't tell from a persons' handwriting. 4. From psychological conditions like high blood pressure and Schizophrenia to personality traits like dominance and aggression: if you write by hand, graphologists can analyse you. 5. Everything from the size of your letters to how closely you space words can reveal intricate details of your personality. In general, the size of your letters can reveal whether you are shy or outgoing. Compared to standard lined sheet of paper, if you write with tiny letters that do not reach the top line, you are likely to have a timid and introverted personality. If you write with large letters that go over the topline. You are likely to be opposite: outgoing, confident and attention seeking. 6. Studies suggest that people who space words widely like freedom and independence, whereas those choosing to write with small spaces prefer to be among others and do not like to be alone.

- **(c)** Clarify
- **(d)** Confirm

**उत्तर: (d)**

Verify – प्रमाणित करना, साबित करना, जिसका correct similar word 'confirm' –पुष्टि करना होगा। अन्य शब्दों के अर्थ हैं– Notify - सूचना देना Discover - खोजबीन करना, Clarify - स्पष्ट करना

- **(d)** Repeal

Reveal - खुलासा, प्रकट का correct opposite word 'conceal' - छिपाना, गुप्त रखना, परदा करना होगा। अन्य शब्दों के अर्थ– Teal - नील हरित रंग Blacken - बदनाम करना, कलंक लागाना Repeal - खंडन करना, रद्द करना

### प्रश्न 123

Which part of speech is the underlined word in the following sentence? Graphologists can verify whether the autographs are real or fake?

- **(a)** Pronoun
- **(b)** Conjunctions
- **(c)** Adverb
- **(d)** Preposition

**उत्तर: (b)**

दिये गये वाक्य में 'or' का प्रयोग as a conjunction हुआ है। क्यूंकि 'OR' यहाँ दो शब्दों 'Real' और 'fake' को conjuct कर रहा है। Conjunction–Conjunction are words which connect sentences or groups of words. Ex: but, or, so, and, yet etc.

> **निर्देश / गद्यांश:** Directions : Read the passage given below carefully and answer the questions that follow (Q. Nos. 129 to 135) by selecting the correct/most appropriate options. 1. There is something we all want to do, although few of us readily admit it: Get rid of guests. 2. For nine months in the year, only my closest friends come to see me. Then, when temperatures start soaring in the plains, long-lost acquaintances suddenly remember that I exist, and people whom I am barely able to recognize appear at the front door, willing to have me put them up for periods ranging from six days to six week. 3. Occasionally, I am the master of the situation I inform them that the cottage is already bursting, that people are sleeping on the floor. If the hopefuls start looking around for signs of these uncomfortable guests, I remark that they have all gone out for a picnic. 4. The other day I received visitors who proved to be more thick-skinned than most. The man was a friend of a friend of an acquaintance of mine. I had never seen him before. But on the strength of this distance relationship, he had brought his family along. 5. I tried the usual play but it didn't work. The man and his family were perfectly willing to share the floor with any others who might be staying with me. 6. So I made my next move. ' I must warn you about the scorpions', I said. The scorpion-scare is effective with most people. But I was dealing with professionals. The man set his son rolling up the carpet. 'Sometimes centipedes fall from the ceiling', I said desperately. 7. We were now interrupted by someone knocking on the front door. It was the postman with a rejected manuscript, his arrival inspired me to greater inventiveness. 8. 'I' m terribly sorry', I said, staring hard at a rejection slip. 'I'm afraid I have to leave immediately. A paper wants me to interview the Maharishi. I hope you won't mind. Would you like the name of a good hotel?' 9. 'Oh, don't worry about us', said the woman expansively. 'We'll look after the house while you are away.'
